# Write NONMEM input formatted nif object to file

Write as space-delimited, fixed-width or a character-separated file. If
the 'numeric_fields_only' argument is TRUE, only columns that are
numeric will be exported.

## Usage

``` r
write_nonmem(
  obj,
  filename = NULL,
  fields = NULL,
  sep = NULL,
  numeric_fields_only = FALSE,
  dot_columns = NULL
)
```

## Arguments

- obj:

  The NIF object.

- filename:

  The filename as string. If not filename is specified, the file is
  printed only.

- fields:

  The fields to export in addition to the minimally required fields. If
  NULL (default), all fields will be exported.

- sep:

  The separating character, e.g. ',' or ';'. If NULL (default), the
  output has a space-separated, fixed-width format.

- numeric_fields_only:

  Include only numerical fields.

- dot_columns:

  Fields in which zeros will be replaced with ".".

## Value

Nothing.

## Details

All numeric fields are reduced to 4 decimal places. For this, IEC 60559
is applied, i.e., rounding for a last digit of 5 is to the next even
number. All fields are converted to character, and NA-values are
converted to '.'.

Further, in the columns specified by the 'dot_columns' argument, zero
values are also converted to '.'.

The records are written in the order of the nif object. This matters
because NONMEM does not re-sort records but processes them in file
order. In a nif object, a sample at the same time as a dose is placed
before the dose (a trough, relating to the previous dose), see
[`nif()`](nif.md). No re-ordering takes place when writing.

## Examples

``` r
write_nonmem(examplinib_fe_nif)
#> REF ID STUDYID    USUBJID           AGE SEX RACE                      HEIGHT WEIGHT BMI     DTC                 TIME  NTIME TAFD  TAD EVID AMT CMT DV        ANALYTE PARENT TRTDY METABOLITE DOSE MDV ACTARMCD IMPUTATION               EPOCH                  PERIOD TREATMENT FASTED

#> 2   1  2023000400 20230004001010002 43  1   WHITE                     183.8  73.3   21.6977 2001-01-12 09:47:00 0     0     0     0   1    500 1   .         RS2023  RS2023 1     FALSE      500  1   BA       time copied from EXSTDTC OPEN LABEL TREATMENT 1 1      B         0
```
